<div class="eyebrow">#01 · <span class='en'>Today's top story</span><span class='zh'>今日头条</span></div><h2><span class='en'>Tech Companies Are Trying to Shift the Concept of Privacy (Again)</span><span class='zh'>科技公司又在试图改变隐私的定义</span></h2>
<div class="alt"><span class='zh'>Tech Companies Are Trying to Shift the Concept of Privacy (Again)</span></div><p><span class='en'>Privacy is increasingly affecting people beyond the individual who owns or uses a device. Meta and Google’s smart glasses can record what the wearer is looking at or use AI to describe it, extending the consequences of data collection to bystanders. The shift challenges the older framing of privacy as primarily a matter of personal choice.</span><span class='zh'>隐私问题正越来越多地影响设备所有者或使用者之外的人。Meta 和 Google 的智能眼镜可以记录佩戴者所看到的内容，或使用 AI 对其进行描述，使数据收集的后果波及旁观者。这种变化挑战了过去主要将隐私视为个人选择的传统定义。</span></p>

<p><span class='en'>The documentary tells the story of KDE, an early open-source community that offered computer users a free and open alternative to proprietary desktops such as Windows.</span><span class='zh'>这部纪录片讲述了 KDE 这一早期开源社区的故事，以及它如何为计算机用户提供 Windows 等专有桌面的免费、开放替代方案。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://gitlab.com/snipedbywifi/terminactory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">07</div><div><h4><span class='en'>Show HN: A terminal-native idle factory game, written in Rust with ratatui</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 Rust 和 ratatui 编写的终端原生放置工厂游戏</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>Term inactory is a cozy factory game that runs directly in a terminal and was built for playing while waiting on long-running agent tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Term inactory 是一款直接运行在终端中的休闲工厂游戏，适合在等待耗时较长的代理任务时游玩。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://monkeyisland.pages.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">08</div><div><h4><span class='en'>Monkey Island: all 13 versions side by side</span><span class='zh'>《猴岛的秘密》全部 13 个版本并排对比</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>The project presents every version of The Secret of Monkey Island from 1990 side by side, covering eight editions and 13 display modes across systems including Hercules, CGA, EGA, VGA, Amiga, Atari ST, Macintosh, FM Towns, and Sega CD.</span><span class='zh'>这个项目并排展示了 1990 年《猴岛的秘密》的所有版本，涵盖 Hercules、CGA、EGA、VGA、Amiga、Atari ST、Macintosh、FM Towns 和 Sega CD 等平台上的 8 个版本、13 种显示模式。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://github.com/ggml-org/ggml/releases/tag/v1.0.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">09</div><div><h4><span class='en'>Ggml v1.0.0</span><span class='zh'>ggml v1.0.0</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>ggml 1.0.0 removes deprecated public APIs and the legacy DIAG_MASK operations, while adding configurable YaRN truncation and FP8 support.</span><span class='zh'>ggml 1.0.0 移除了已弃用的公共 API 和旧版 DIAG_MASK 操作，并加入可配置的 YaRN 截断和 FP8 支持。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://research.google/blog/advancing-science-engineering-with-antigravitys-teamwork-feature/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">10</div><div><h4><span class='en'>Advancing science and engineering with Antigravity&#x27;s /teamwork</span><span class='zh'>用 Antigravity 的 /teamwork 推进科学与工程</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>Google Research reports recent results from Antigravity’s /teamwork multi-agent systems, which are designed to support human-AI collaboration on open problems.</span><span class='zh'>Google Research 公布了 Antigravity 的 /teamwork 多代理系统的最新成果，该系统旨在支持人类与 AI 协作解决开放性问题。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://github.com/paul-nameless/agweb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">11</div><div><h4><span class='en'>Show HN: Agweb – a browser for agents that stays out of your way</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Agweb——不妨碍用户操作的代理浏览器</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>Agweb is a macOS browser for coding agents, built on WebKit and designed not to steal keyboard focus.</span><span class='zh'>Agweb 是一款面向编码代理的 macOS 浏览器，基于 WebKit 构建，设计目标是不抢占键盘焦点。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://www.joshka.net/cell-borders/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">12</div><div><h4><span class='en'>Terminal borders that don&#x27;t cost a cell</span><span class='zh'>不占用终端单元格的边框</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>A proposed terminal protocol draws thin borders, rounded corners, and gridlines along cell edges without consuming rows or columns.</span><span class='zh'>一种新的终端协议可以沿单元格边缘绘制细线、圆角和网格线，而不占用额外的行或列。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://foss.life/notes/2026/the-myth-of-neutral-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">13</div><div><h4><span class='en'>The Myth of Neutral Code</span><span class='zh'>中立代码的神话</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>The essay challenges the corporate idea that open-source software is apolitical, neutral, and governed purely by technical merit.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章质疑一种企业化观念：开源软件是非政治的、中立的，并且完全由技术 merit 统治。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://orcasairband.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">14</div><div><h4><span class='en'>Live airband on all frequencies from KORS. Victoria, CA also ground stopped</span><span class='zh'>KORS 全频段直播航空无线电；加拿大维多利亚也实施地面停飞</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>The service provides live, transcribed, and archived aviation radio transmissions from Orcas Island, Washington.</span><span class='zh'>这项服务提供来自华盛顿州奥卡斯岛的航空无线电实时转录和存档内容。</span></p></div></a><a class="row" href="https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/nov/13/kurt-godel-notebooks-ai-helps-unlock-hidden-discoveries-man-who-transformed-maths" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">15</div><div><h4><span class='en'>AI unlocks Gödel&#x27;s notebooks, said to contain hundreds of proved theorems</span><span class='zh'>AI 解读哥德尔笔记本，据称其中包含数百个已证明定理</span></h4>

<section class="codesec"><div class="sech"><h2><span class='en'>Open source this week</span><span class='zh'>本周开源项目</span> <span class='hsub'><span class='en'>GitHub · GitLab · Hugging Face … from the past 7 days</span><span class='zh'>过去 7 天 GitHub、GitLab、Hugging Face 等链接</span></span></h2>

<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>anthropics/claude-code</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Claude Code sends your email address to websites</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 会将你的邮箱地址发送给网站</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>A reported Claude Code bug exposed a user&#x27;s email address to arbitrary third parties in the User-Agent header during web searches.</span><span class='zh'>有用户报告称，Claude Code 在进行网页搜索时，会通过 User-Agent 请求头将其邮箱地址暴露给任意第三方。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 5</span><span class='zh'>11月5日</span> · ▲ 1605</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/aws/aws-emulator" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">02</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>aws/aws-emulator</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>AWS emulator</span><span class='zh'>AWS 模拟器</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>AWS Emulator is presented as a project related to emulating AWS services.</span><span class='zh'>AWS Emulator 是一个用于模拟 AWS 服务的项目。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span> · ▲ 967</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/ppmpreetham/BUI" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">03</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>ppmpreetham/BUI</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Blender&#x27;s UI and Runtime: Much Faster Than GPUI</span><span class='zh'>Blender 的 UI 与运行时：比 GPUI 快得多</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>BUI is a GPU-accelerated Blender user interface decoupled from Blender&#x27;s original codebase.</span><span class='zh'>BUI 是一个与 Blender 原始代码库解耦的 GPU 加速用户界面。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span> · ▲ 726</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/simpsoka/zigzag" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">04</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>simpsoka/zigzag</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Show HN: My $300 embroidery machine needed a subscription, so I built my own app</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我的 300 美元刺绣机需要订阅，于是我自己写了应用</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>A developer built a replacement app for a $300 embroidery machine that required a subscription.</span><span class='zh'>一名开发者为一台需要订阅的 300 美元刺绣机编写了替代应用。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span> · ▲ 719</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/thisisme6598/gemini_files/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">05</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>thisisme6598/gemini_files</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Google Gemini routes personal Drive files and emails to human annotators</span><span class='zh'>Google Gemini 将个人 Drive 文件和邮件交由人工标注员处理</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>Updated Google guidance reportedly confirms that content from connected Drive and email accounts can enter human-review pipelines for Gemini.</span><span class='zh'>Google 更新后的说明据称确认，连接到 Gemini 的 Drive 和电子邮件账户内容可能进入人工审核流程。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 9</span><span class='zh'>11月9日</span> · ▲ 716</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/janestreet/agent_skills/blob/master/socratic-tutor/SKILL.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">06</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>janestreet/agent_skills</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Socratic Tutor</span><span class='zh'>苏格拉底式导师</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>The Socratic Tutor is an agent skill designed to guide users toward genuine mastery through questions that make them reason, predict, and explain rather than simply listen to a lecture.</span><span class='zh'>“苏格拉底式导师”是一项代理技能，通过要求用户推理、预测和解释来帮助其真正掌握知识，而不是单纯进行讲授。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span> · ▲ 655</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/PrivSec-dev/banking-apps-compat-report/issues/105" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">07</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>PrivSec-dev/banking-apps-compat-report</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Dutch &#x27;idealist&#x27; bank ASN blocks non-Google Android, including GrapheneOS</span><span class='zh'>荷兰“理想主义”银行 ASN 屏蔽非 Google Android，包括 GrapheneOS</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>A compatibility report examines whether the ASN Bank Android app blocks non-Google Android systems, including GrapheneOS.</span><span class='zh'>一份兼容性报告调查 ASN Bank 的 Android 应用是否屏蔽包括 GrapheneOS 在内的非 Google Android 系统。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 6</span><span class='zh'>11月6日</span> · ▲ 648</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">08</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>hyperionaidev/smolmaxxing</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>I&#x27;m making LLMs work on small and old devices</span><span class='zh'>让大语言模型运行在小型和老旧设备上</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>smolmaxxing explores how to run the largest practical language models on small or old devices such as TV boxes, phones, single-board computers, and routers.</span><span class='zh'>smolmaxxing 探索如何在电视盒子、旧手机、单板计算机和路由器等小型或老旧设备上运行实际可用的最大语言模型。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span> · ▲ 466</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">09</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>GoogleChrome/modern-web-guidance</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Show HN: Get your coding agent off ~2019 JavaScript, onto modern web primitives</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：让编码代理摆脱约 2019 年的 JavaScript，转向现代 Web 原语</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>A Chrome-led project provides coding agents with curated guidance on modern, native web APIs.</span><span class='zh'>一个由 Chrome 发起的项目为编码代理提供现代原生 Web API 的精选指南。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 10</span><span class='zh'>11月10日</span> · ▲ 423</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/synchopate/llama-modchip" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">10</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>synchopate/llama-modchip</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Llama-modchip: 284B DeepSeek on 64GB RAM, no GPU</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip：仅用 64GB 内存、无需 GPU 运行 284B DeepSeek</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>Llama-modchip is a collection of ggml and llama.cpp kernel patches, quantization transcoders, and runtime tools for running sparse MoE models entirely in system RAM.</span><span class='zh'>Llama-modchip 是一组 ggml 和 llama.cpp 内核补丁、量化转换器及运行时工具，旨在让稀疏 MoE 模型完全运行于系统内存中。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 8</span><span class='zh'>11月8日</span> · ▲ 404</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://codeberg.org/wvhulle/bootvine" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">11</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=codeberg.org&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>wvhulle/bootvine</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>A live tree of systemd jobs on the boot console</span><span class='zh'>启动控制台上的 systemd 作业实时树</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>bootvine is a boot animation that displays systemd stages as a tree.</span><span class='zh'>bootvine 是一款启动动画，会将 systemd 的各个阶段显示为一棵树。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 12</span><span class='zh'>11月12日</span> · ▲ 360</span></div></a><a class="row crow" href="https://github.com/FrigadeHQ/yogurt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class="n">12</div><div>
<div class="repo"><div class='src'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=32' alt='' loading='lazy'><span>FrigadeHQ/yogurt</span></div></div><h4><span class='en'>Show HN: Yogurt – Free, private, open-source alternative to Granola</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Yogurt——免费、私密的开源 Granola 替代品</span></h4>
<p><span class='en'>Yogurt is a free, MIT-licensed desktop app that uses on-device AI models to transcribe, summarize, and chat about meetings.</span><span class='zh'>Yogurt 是一款采用 MIT 许可证的免费桌面应用，使用设备本地的 AI 模型转录会议、生成摘要并进行问答。</span></p><span class='rd'><span class='en'>Nov 11</span><span class='zh'>11月11日</span> · ▲ 285</span></div></a></div></section>
